- Consolidated class **RT-01 — Android private artifact publication**: every affected artifact/recovery/composer test fails at `AndroidAtomicArtifactPublisher.publish` because it uses `Os.link` and Android returns `EACCES`. Replace the Android hard-link strategy with a private-storage-supported atomic publish primitive that preserves the repository contract (final name appears atomically; an existing target is never replaced). Positive and pre-existing-target controls must execute against the Android filesystem, and the test inventory must cover every repository operation that shares this publisher. No DB publication intent may be lost on a failed publish.
- Consolidated class **RT-02 — OpenCV native loading**: the resolved `org.opencv:opencv:4.14.0` AAR includes ABI libraries named `libopencv_java4.so`, while `Core.NATIVE_LIBRARY_NAME` resolves to `opencv_java4140`; `DocumentCropProcessor` therefore fails before either automatic or manual crop runs. Load the native library actually packaged by the pinned dependency and exercise both crop entry paths plus the no-candidate positive/negative controls on Android.
- Consolidated class **RT-03 — PDF renderer test oracle**: `SavedPdfPageRenderer` caps output at a 1600px long side, but Android `PdfRenderer.Page.width/height` may already be below that bound (the A4 fixture yielded a max dimension of 1190px). The assertion of equality to 1600 is invalid; assert positive dimensions and `max(width,height) <= MAX_LONG_SIDE_PX`, then verify page index/count, landscape orientation, and corrupt/out-of-range rejection. Do not enlarge valid pages to satisfy a test-only equality.
- Contract for product `0.4.71` / versionCode 190: implement and document fixes for all three classes, with class-level regression coverage and no owner decision or gate/status changes. Acceptance requires the complete connected Android instrumentation suite to pass on the recorded AVD, all core and host checks plus SQLDelight migration and debug/androidTest/release builds to pass via `tools/check_all.sh --rerun`, signed candidate metadata/checksum and version-bound evidence to be recorded, and no regressions in existing fail-closed storage/recovery cases. Execution refresh (revision 5.62, 2026-09-24) — S1 schema helper idempotency defect and contract before implementation:
- The saved `0.1.6` S6 logs contain repeated SQLite errors `duplicate column name: state in "ALTER TABLE SaveManifest ADD COLUMN state TEXT;"`. Source tracing shows `onCreate` creates `SaveManifest.state`, then `ensureS6Tables` calls `safeAddColumn` which executes the same ALTER on every new S1 database; `safeAddColumn` catches every exception, hiding both this known duplicate and unrelated migration failures. This masks real schema errors and pollutes each run's diagnostics.
- Contract for app harness `0.1.7` / versionCode `8`: `safeAddColumn` must inspect `PRAGMA table_info` and execute ALTER only when the named column is absent; identifiers must be constrained to known safe SQL identifier syntax. Do not swallow SQL errors from a required ALTER. Preserve fresh-create and v1→v2 migration semantics and existing data. Add separate Android instrumentation cases for repeated `ensureS6Tables` and v1→v2 upgrade, checking exactly one `SaveManifest.state`, all required tables/columns and preserved Page row. Acceptance requires the full app connected suite (23 tests, zero failures), S6's 123 DEV runs with **zero duplicate-column messages**, app debug/androidTest builds, host tests, the full local runner, and versioned evidence/hashes. Execution refresh (revision 5.80, 2026-09-24) — close missing-Workspace bootstrap path discovered in full-class audit:

Execution refresh (revision 5.99, 2026-09-24) — RT-08 Android retry test completion oracle:
- Full Android run at candidate `0.4.89` / code `208` (source `89da721`) failed `TryAgainImportActivityInstrumentedTest.retryImportsAllPagesSuppressesConcurrentDuplicateAndPreservesSource`: after an expected second deliberate retry, Page count stayed 4 rather than 6 for 30 seconds; terminal status showed `Text recognition complete`. Source audit of the test helper found that `awaitRetryHandlerIdle` accepted any status containing `2` and “import/page”, including the in-progress message “page 2 of 2”. It could therefore return before the handler's `finally` released its concurrent duplicate guard; the next intentional retry was ignored. This is RT-08, a test-oracle defect (source inference corroborated by the failure), not evidence of a product import defect.
- Contract for `0.4.90` / code `209`: make the Activity test await the exact localized successful completion status for two imported pages (and ensure the main-thread completion callback has returned) before issuing the later deliberate retry. Keep page counts, retry/duplicate identity assertions, source hash checks and production behavior unchanged. Do not weaken the timeout or success expectations.
- Acceptance: targeted Try-again Activity test passes three runs; full connected suite passes all 47 tests; core/local runner and release build checks pass; signed `.89`→`.90` upgrade retains Page 1. Preserve the initial failing run as evidence and capture post-fix XML/UTP/logcat/checksums. Product MVP/gate statuses are not changed by correcting the test oracle.

Execution refresh (revision 5.106, 2026-09-24) — T8.3 successful editor-path defect found:
- Evidence from the first real UI positive run at product `0.4.93`: normal two-page Save and editor navigation/reorder completed, but editor Save displayed `Edited PDF was not confirmed: Edited PDF page order is malformed`; no edited Recent output was confirmed. The previous denial/revocation evidence at `.92` remains valid for its separate fail-closed path. The accepted `.93` positive-path contract is NOT YET SATISFIED.
- Source audit found the full defect class: Kotlin `joinToString(prefix="[", postfix="]")` writes comma-space page arrays, while both `StateStore.commitLocalExportArtifact` and `editedPdfOutputPageCount` parse comma-separated numbers without trimming. A two-page order such as `[1, 0]` therefore fails. Single-page `[0]` does not expose it.
- Remediation is recorded as `BUG-PDF-EDIT-ORDER-01`: write canonical comma-separated indexes without optional spaces and trim each parsed token in both durable commit validation and Recent page-count projection. Add a core regression for two-page `[1,0]` commit and a successful Android editor UI regression verifying output identity, provider read-back, page order, new Recent entry and unchanged original bytes. This is a code defect fix; no T8.3 behavior, DR-19–21 decision or acceptance threshold changes.
- Step accepted only when core order serialization/round-trip regression passes, the product `.93` successful editor UI workflow passes twice after the fix, `.92` revoke negative and normal Save controls stay green, complete connected suite passes without failure/skip, local runner and signed release upgrade pass, and the original source remains byte-identical. Preserve the failed UI report/log and subsequent success evidence separately.

Execution contract (revision 5.119, 2026-09-24) — T5.2/T5.6 private PDF source publication intent and recovery:
- Source audit at `b3cfebe` / product `0.4.98` / code `217`: `ProductStorage.importPdf` durably publishes the copied PDF to `objects/<prefix>/<ImportSessionId>.pdf`, then parses/validates it, and only afterwards calls `StateStore.beginPdfImport`, which atomically registers the ArtifactRecord and ImportSession. A process death after the file rename but before that SQL transaction leaves a deterministic private PDF with no database owner; startup recovery queries only ArtifactRecord rows and cannot discover it. A synchronous malformed/encrypted/unsupported PDF likewise fails after publication and before metadata registration. `ENG-PDF-IMPORT-05/06` cover page-image publication and transaction boundaries, not this source-PDF boundary. The related `SaveOutputPublicationIntent` pattern proves the project already uses durable DB intent-before-publication for owned immutable outputs.
- Contract `ENG-PDF-IMPORT-07`: add a bounded SQL publication intent keyed by the deterministic ImportSessionId before reading/copying the input; persist workspace and display-name context, but never the SAF URI or a persistable provider grant. After private publication and successful PDF inspection, one transaction must create/verify the source ArtifactRecord, create/verify the ImportSession, attach its recovery reference and remove the intent. Startup must reconcile only intents owned by this importer: a present valid PDF is promoted to the normal resumable ImportSession and then processed through the existing Activity recovery path; an absent/partially copied source is abandoned with only its ID-prefixed private staging files removed; a present invalid PDF is removed by exact private ID after path/symlink/ownership checks, then the intent is removed. A synchronous copy/inspection failure must attempt the same exact cleanup and retain the intent if cleanup cannot be proven complete. No cleanup may inspect or delete provider/user files, unrelated artifact IDs, committed ArtifactRecords, or active import-session files. Repeated startup/retry must converge; the same import identity with changed bytes or conflicting workspace/display context must fail closed. Schema forward migration and database transaction boundaries are part of the implementation.

The default full suite must never pause/kill the app. Capture source commit/product version, PIDs, intent/DB snapshots, file paths and SHA-256 values before/after recovery, XML/UTP/logcat, migration results, APK identity and checksums under `execution/device_tests/0.4.99-current-api36/`.

Keep the previous API36.1 `.98` capture as an explicit device compatibility observation. Preserve failed attempts in evidence. Build completion or a simulated Activity restart is not process-death evidence.

It does not close provider revocation during source copy, low-storage behavior, the 230-case crash matrix, FACT-07, PDF content/render quality, physical Xiaomi/provider qualification, independent review or owner acceptance. Execution contract (revision 5.121, 2026-09-24) — T5.2 SAF source failure/revocation boundary:
- Source audit at `9f14ce8` / product `0.4.99` / code `218`: `ProductActivity.importSelectedDocuments` reads the selected URI through `ContentResolver.openInputStream` and passes the live stream to `ProductStorage.importPdf`. The storage layer persists an ID-bound `PdfImportPublicationIntent`, copies and publishes an immutable private PDF, validates it, and then creates the resumable ImportSession. On synchronous copy/inspection failure it attempts exact private publication cleanup and intent abandonment; cleanup uncertainty is retained. Once publication completes, page inspection/materialization reads the private snapshot, not the selected URI. Existing `.99` evidence tests process death after publication and malformed input, but does not exercise a real granted ContentProvider stream failing partway through copy or OS grant revocation while that stream is active.
- Contract `ENG-PDF-IMPORT-08`: add an isolated Android test ContentProvider and use its real `content://` URI and temporary read grant with the production `ProductStorage.importPdf`/`ContentResolver` stream boundary. A deterministic provider stream must emit a known PDF prefix and then fail during copy. Require import failure, no final PDF object, no ID-prefixed staging object after cleanup/restart, no ArtifactRecord/ImportSession/Page, and unchanged unrelated sentinel bytes/rows. A positive control imports a complete provider PDF and verifies the private source SHA-256 and ordered pages. In a separate positive case, revoke the exact test-owned read grant after the private source is published but before PDF inspection continues; the fresh URI must no longer open, while import must finish from the private snapshot with the expected source hash/pages and no additional provider open/read. During-copy revocation must be injected after the provider confirms bytes have been read; record whether the already-open descriptor completes or fails on the tested Android build. Either result is acceptable only if a complete exact snapshot is imported or a failed copy leaves no published/registered partial source and recovery converges. Never revoke a pre-existing grant, enumerate or mutate a user/provider tree, persist the source URI, or change import policy/statuses.
- Acceptance step: at product `0.4.100` / code `219`, the committed source has (a) two repeated actual ContentProvider mid-copy failure runs with exact pre/post SQL/filesystem assertions and a second-startup cleanup check, (b) two repeated actual grant-revocation-during-active-copy runs with provider open/read trace and either exact-hash complete import or clean failure as above, (c) two post-publication revocation runs proving the resolver denies a new open and import resumes solely from the private source, and (d) positive complete-source controls. Fixture IDs, package/authority, grant lifecycle, byte counts/hashes, ordered page identities, database snapshots, provider event traces, XML/UTP/logcat and cleanup results must be captured under `execution/device_tests/0.4.100-current-api36/`; delete only fixture-owned DB/artifact files and release only grants acquired by the test.
- Run the exact committed-source complete connected instrumentation suite on API 36.1 with zero failures/errors/skips, `./tools/check_all.sh --rerun` including core/host/migration/debug/androidTest/release/traceability/task/evidence checks, and signed `.99`→`.100` AVD upgrade preserving the existing seeded Page 1. Record command, environment, source commit, product version/code, result, APK hash/signature, and evidence links. Preserve failed runs. If emulator/provider behavior differs from the allowed deterministic outcomes, investigate and adjust implementation/tests without weakening the stated state invariants. No task/gate/threshold or MVP status change is authorized. Physical Xiaomi/provider qualification, wider crash matrix, FACT-07, independent review and owner acceptance remain open; T5.2/T5.6/CAP-002 remain PARTIAL and MVP remains `IN_PROGRESS_NOT_ACCEPTED`.

Execution contract (revision 5.124, 2026-09-24) — recover image imports left OPEN by product 0.4.100:
- Follow-up audit: `StateStore.beginImageImport` at `.100` commits the `DOCUMENT_PAGE` ArtifactRecord and OPEN ImportSession with a RECOVERY source reference, then `commitImportedImage` separately commits Page/ImageVersion and marks the session complete. A real process death between those transactions leaves a valid private image plus an OPEN session but no Page/ImageVersion identity; the old startup path only resumes PDF sessions. A forward-only `.101` change must preserve and complete such existing `.100` user data.
- Contract `ENG-IMAGE-IMPORT-02`: on startup, inventory bounded OPEN one-page ImportSessions whose source snapshot ArtifactRecord is `DOCUMENT_PAGE`. For these legacy rows only, verify the exact private file against stored path/size/SHA, decode it, parse the persisted MIME/display-name payload, and create a durable image publication intent using the existing ImportSessionId/ArtifactId plus deterministic PageId/ImageVersionId derived from that session. Atomically materialize one Page/ImageVersion, complete the ImportSession, move the source reference to the authoritative ImageVersion and remove the temporary intent. Repeated startup must resolve to the same IDs and no duplicates. Execution contract (revision 5.130, 2026-09-24) — ENG-CLEANUP-02 duplicate-worker exclusion:
- Code audit of `CleanupArbiter.runBatch` and `recoverInterruptedDeletions` found that a worker lists every durable `GC_CLAIMED` row and may release/finalize it while another worker is between filesystem unlink and SQL finalization. Since unlink runs outside a DB transaction, a second scheduled worker can misclassify an active claim as interrupted; the first worker can then encounter a stale claim while finalizing. Durable claim tokens protect stale writes but do not identify an active worker.
- Contract `ENG-CLEANUP-02`: serialize the full cleanup critical section (interrupted-claim inspection/recovery, new claim acquisition, checksum-verified unlink, and DB finalization/quarantine) across threads and processes sharing the app-private artifact root. Coordination must be released by normal completion, exception, thread interruption or process death; it must not depend on wall-clock expiry or guess at whether a worker is alive. Preserve durable GC claim/restart semantics, reference checks, exact-path/checksum validation and fail-closed outcomes. The lock artifact may not be treated as a user document or eligible cleanup target.
- Acceptance for product `0.4.106` / code `225`: add a deterministic JVM regression that pauses worker A after the private object unlink but before directory-sync/SQL finalization, starts worker B, and proves B cannot recover/finalize the active claim while A holds the cleanup lock. After releasing A, both workers must finish without exception; the artifact row must be `DELETED`, the object absent, each invocation must report at most one deletion total, and no reference may be lost. Also verify lock release after an injected failure and that existing before/after-unlink recovery, corruption quarantine and reference-protection tests remain green. Run `tools/check_all.sh --rerun`, the connected API 36.1 suite, signed `.105`→`.106` data-retention upgrade, and record artifact identity/checksums. Do not change retention duration, CONFLICT-01/DR-23, task/gate/CAP statuses or MVP acceptance. Cross-process Android process-death qualification and owner/independent review remain separate.

Execution contract (revision 5.167, 2026-09-25) — ENG-ACTIVE-VIEWER-01 complete CAP-011 cross-view state:
- Source audit at `9056585` / product `0.4.122` / code `241`, against Functional Map §§11, 17, 18 and UFS CAP-011 BR-011-01…06 / AC-011-01…04: workspace thumbnail taps open Viewer but do not persist Active until image decoding succeeds; workspace thumbnails have no Active white frame; Viewer has no synchronized thumbnail strip, per-thumbnail Selected indicator/control, or horizontal page swipe. Previous/Next buttons exist and stop at the first/last item. This leaves Active/Selected cross-view behavior materially incomplete.
- Required implementation: use one ordered `pageViewerPages` snapshot as the Viewer strip and navigation source. Tapping any workspace/Viewer thumbnail immediately sets durable Active without changing Selected. Render an unmistakable white frame on the Active thumbnail and a separate native checkbox for Selected in both workspace rows and the Viewer strip. Checkbox changes persist only that Page's Selected value and refresh both views. Horizontal swipe over the main Viewer Page navigates one Page in the gesture direction, updates durable Active, main image/text/title, strip frame and visible scroll position together. Keep Previous/Next behavior aligned; no wrap at edges. Active must persist even when image decode fails. Dispose decoded bitmaps on replacement/close, and ignore stale asynchronous loads.
- Authority/decision: product behavior is required by the frozen Functional Map and UFS; DR-16 remains unchecked. The user directly delegated engineering questions to the system engineer (plan §4 / 2026-09-24), so `execution/decisions.md` records the minimal boundary default “stop at edge without wrapping”, already represented by disabled Previous/Next controls. Do not implement the DR-16 suggested spring animation or mark DR-16 accepted; that optional owner preference remains open. No other owner decision or acceptance threshold changes.
- Inventory and class search: inspect all Workspace Page thumb activation, Viewer index transitions, selection controls, active-state rendering and image decode completion in `ProductActivity`; inspect `ProductStorage.setActivePage`, `WorkspaceProjection.activePageId`, and core state-store active/selection transactions. Search all UI call sites using `setActivePage` and all workspace thumbnail controls; saved-PDF editor and Recent PDF viewer are separate CAPs and are regression-check boundaries, not implementation targets. Negative classes: decode failure, stale/late decode after rapid navigation, swipe at both edges, tapping Active does not select/deselect, toggling Selected does not move Active, and page reorder/refresh keeps strip order and ordinal labels aligned.
- Acceptance for product `0.4.123` / code `242`: add Android instrumentation on API 36.1 with at least three persisted fixture Pages and mixed selection. Prove workspace Active frame initially matches projection; tapping Page 2 opens Viewer and durably makes only Page 2 Active; Viewer strip order/frame and per-Page check state agree with storage; Viewer checkbox toggles Page 2 selection without changing Active; tapping Page 3 strip item updates displayed Page and Active; swipes in each direction update main page, frame and Active; Previous/Next agree; first/last controls and swipes do not wrap; corrupt-image decode still leaves chosen Page Active; rapid navigation cannot display stale bitmap or move Active backward; closing/reopening returns to durable Active. Ensure fixture cleanup restores all test-owned rows. Run targeted instrumented scenarios repeatedly in English and Russian, full connected suite with zero failures/errors/skips, configured `tools/check_all.sh --rerun`, and signed `.122`→`.123` API36.1 upgrade retaining workspace state. Preserve exact commands, environment/version, XML/logcat, screenshots/state, APK/signature hashes and raw bundle manifest; evidence goes in `execution/evidence/T3.7-active-viewer-0.4.123.md` and `execution/device_tests/0.4.123-current-api36/`.

Execution-contract correction (revision 5.232, 2026-09-25) — ENG-SAF-GRANT-DEATH-01 surfaced BUG-SAF-STATUS-01:
- The first `.148` host-driven process-death run reached both real grant checkpoints and killed both target app processes. Repeat 1 passed grant persistence/revalidation and the post-revocation negative check. Repeat 2 retained the exact persisted URI and `SafDestinationGrantManager.revalidate` succeeded, but the integration test could not observe the expected provider-not-qualified UI status before timeout. Both attempts, PID/checkpoint data, phase logs and fixture cleanup are preserved in `execution/device_tests/0.4.148-current-api36/saf-grant-process-death-attempt-1-status-race/`.
- Source audit found `ProductActivity.startCamera` asynchronously overwrites `status.text` with Camera ready on success (and Camera error on failure) without checking whether a newer action status appeared while CameraX initialized. This explains the observed status race; no URI-grant or Save-qualification failure was observed. Fix the shared async status source by writing a camera completion message only if the visible status still matches the value captured when camera initialization began. This prevents camera startup from erasing newer SAF and other action feedback while still showing camera success/error when no newer state supersedes it. Do not alter SAF policy, qualification criteria, permissions, Saved Page state, task statuses or thresholds.
- Retain the original revision 5.231 acceptance conditions at `.148`: two actual process deaths/resumes must pass positive persistence and post-revocation checks; then full suite, committed-source local checks and signed `.147`→`.148` state retention must pass. Corrected test output must report the observed status on failure. Earlier `.148` attempt remains diagnostic, not accepted evidence.

Execution contract amendment (revision 5.254, 2026-09-25) — ENG-IMAGE-PICKER-MALFORMED-JPEG-DECODE-CONTINUATION-01:
- Evidence-based correction before implementation: API 36.1 `BitmapFactory` returns a nonnull partial Bitmap for a JPEG with readable frame/SOS headers but no entropy-coded image data; corrupting frame component metadata or Huffman tables causes bounds parsing itself to return `outWidth/outHeight = -1` and no MIME. - Preserve the product scenario and source orders, but bound this contract to a JPEG parser/bounds rejection: use a test-owned `.jpg` with JPEG SOI, JFIF APP0 marker and a truncated APP0 segment. Assert the fixture is not decodable and has no valid bounds/MIME; through real image-only DocumentsUI, verify it produces the expected unsupported-image partial error while one valid PNG persists in either order with the same durable state/source/grant/cleanup invariants.
- This amendment replaces only revision 5.253's demand for readable dimensions plus a full-decode failure and its decoder-specific error assertion. It does not reduce the two-order ProductActivity→DocumentsUI scope, add a product requirement, change product behavior or change acceptance thresholds/task/CAP/gate/MVP status. Do not claim full-decode failure coverage for JPEG. The final bounded observation remains distinct from `.155` random bytes by verifying valid JPEG SOI/JFIF marker framing before the truncated APP0 parser rejection.
- Acceptance remains `.157`/276: focused class 16/16; full API 36.1 suite 110/110; clean committed-source checks/B0 hash verification; signed `.156`→`.157` state-preserving upgrade; clean-source release APK identity; order-specific raw evidence/checksum packet. Execution contract amendment (revision 5.282, 2026-09-26) — ENG-MVP-MIXED-IMPORT-TO-SAF-SAVE-01:
- The `.181`/300 focused diagnostic classified the failure: the three Pages and both completed ImportSessions are present and ordered, PDF current Recognition rows link to each current ImageVersion, while the JPEG Page has `current_recognition_id=null`. Its visible status is `Text recognition complete`; Save/open assertions were not reached. Raw evidence and hashes: `execution/evidence/ENG-MVP-MIXED-IMPORT-TO-SAF-SAVE-0.4.181.md`, packet `results/PRODUCT/ENG-MVP-MIXED-IMPORT-TO-SAF-SAVE-0.4.181/focused/attempt-1/`, manifest SHA-256 `dce16fa07abfec7971d8c0b4b2c55d97b143e3f7b3556fa49069257830fcc6ac`.
- **Class ENG-MVP-MIXED-IMPORT-TO-SAF-SAVE-01-F1 (P/C/E):** source inspection shows `StateStore.commitImageImportPublication` and legacy `commitImportedImage` hard-code `OCR_LATIN_V1` on imported image Pages; the production `ProductActivity.scheduleOcr` submits `OCR_RUS_ENG_TESSERACT_V1`. `StateStore.commitRecognition` rejects a candidate whose desired key differs from the Page's stored key, and `scheduleOcr` intentionally consumes `StoreConflict` for stale work. This mismatch prevents all product-created imported-image OCR candidates from becoming current Recognition; `.181` state matches that path. In-scope analogues are both image-import commit paths and `ProductStorage` normal, pending-intent recovery and legacy-session recovery producers. PDF import already passes `IMPORT_OCR_OPERATION_KEY`; camera capture/editor paths already pass the matching Activity key. Core unit fixtures that deliberately exercise the generic legacy key remain independent controls.
- **Correction scope:** carry an explicit nonblank desired OCR operation key on each `ImportImageCommand`, persist it through both core image commit paths, and supply the production Tesseract key from all three `ProductStorage` normal/recovery producers. Add/extend core persistence-and-commit regression coverage, retain wrong-key/stale-candidate rejection, and rerun the unchanged real picker→mixed import→SAF Save/Recent macro. This is a source behavior correction discovered within the authorized Stage 2 flow; no owner policy, OCR quality threshold, acceptance condition, decision, status or claim is changed.
- **Acceptance:** target `.182`/code `301`; core affected regression and focused macro pass, full API 36.1 connected suite passes with zero failures/errors/skips, configured clean-source `tools/check_all.sh --rerun` passes, release signer/identity is verified and `.179`→`.182` install/open retains existing workspace data on the isolated AVD. Execution contract amendment (revision 5.293, 2026-09-26) — ENG-PDF-RENDER-LIFETIME-01:
- **Authority and exact source:** continue existing T5.5 row under Phase 5 and ENG-PDF-IMPORT-01; checked baseline is `4bf4479` / product `.190` / code `309`. T5.5 already requires first/middle/final success and failure, bounded one-page rendering, at most one decoded page bitmap, unchanged source bytes, visible failures and cleanup. No new product threshold or acceptance criterion is introduced.
- **Source audit:** `AndroidPdfImportReader.renderPages` uses `PDDocument.load(...).use`, reserves the existing 80 MiB scheduler estimate around `PDFRenderer.renderImage`, recycles the returned bitmap in `finally`, syncs the temporary PNG, invokes the consumer after the bitmap scope, and deletes the temporary PNG in an outer `finally`. `ProductStorage.materializePdfImport` records an exception on the open session and rethrows; ProductActivity converts per-source exceptions to visible complete/failed/partial import status. These guarantees are currently inferred from code for failures. Existing `AndroidPdfImportReaderInstrumentedTest` runs a successful two-page render and checks ordering, a 3000-pixel upper bound, reusable text and source SHA, but it does not inject render/consumer failures, assert scratch cleanup/reopen after each failure, observe bitmap recycle before the next page, or assert raster orientation/aspect ratio for the rotated page. The current class separately covers empty, malformed, trailer-truncated and encrypted inspection negatives; this amendment does not reopen that accepted bounded EOF fix or expand the parser corpus.
- **Bounded scope and invariants:** add a narrow PDFBox rasterizer-boundary test seam whose production default calls the same `PDFRenderer.renderImage(index, scale)`. With a generated three-page private fixture, prove the unmodified positive path renders first/middle/final in source order, preserves 90-degree orientation and page aspect ratio under the existing 200 dpi / 3000 px profile, and retains source SHA. Inject the same typed render exception at page 0, 1 and 2; preserve its type/message, prevent later page callbacks, and prove the prior pages (if any) were already recycled and only already-delivered callbacks occurred. Separately inject a consumer/publication-callback exception at each page position; prove each passed temporary file exists only during the callback and is deleted on exit. For every failure case require unchanged source SHA, empty test-owned scratch directory, successful subsequent `inspect` of the same source, and a successful next admission through the same scheduler. Track renderer-returned bitmaps and assert the previous bitmap is recycled before the next bitmap is created and before callback invocation, so no second decoded page bitmap overlaps. No low-storage, real device process-death, provider, 30-PDF FACT-07, or quality threshold is claimed by this slice.
- **Versioned execution and evidence:** advance to product `.191` / code `310` only with these instrumentation changes. Preserve focused API 36.1 XML/UTP/per-test logcat, exact source/device/commands, scratch/source hashes and bitmap/failure observations; run the complete API 36.1 connected suite, exact-source clean `tools/check_all.sh --rerun`, signed release identity/hash verification and `.190`→`.191` in-place update plus force-stop/fresh-launch retention. Store raw attempts/manifests under `results/PRODUCT/ENG-PDF-RENDER-LIFETIME-0.4.191/`, checklist under `execution/device_tests/0.4.191-current-api36/`, and bounded report under `execution/evidence/ENG-PDF-RENDER-LIFETIME-0.4.191.md`.
- **Acceptance and boundary:** accept only when positive first/middle/final ordering/orientation/aspect/source-hash controls and all three render-failure plus all three consumer-failure cases pass with exact exception propagation, no later-page callback, no remaining temp output, parser reopen succeeds, scheduler admission is reusable and bitmap overlap is absent; then require focused and complete connected suites with zero failures/errors/skips, clean-source checks exit 0, matching signed artifact identity, and `.190` workspace/selection retained through update and fresh process. Keep failed/setup attempts distinct. This closes only the named renderer lifetime/failure evidence slice; low storage, oversized-corpus memory/quality, cancellation/process interruption breadth, malformed/encrypted corpus breadth, FACT-07, provider/process-death breadth, physical Xiaomi, independent review, owner acceptance and MVP remain open. Execution result and correction contract (revision 5.299, 2026-09-27) — ENG-PDF-IMPORT-CORPUS-01 / finding F1:
- Product `.195`/314 focused on Android 16/API 36.1 `emulator-5572` ran seven tests; the new corpus method failed on the user-password encrypted fixture because `inspect()` returned `PdfImportInvalidSourceException("Private PDF source is malformed or unreadable")` instead of the existing encrypted-PDF unsupported status. The other six methods passed. This is an observed product classification defect, not a fixture/setup failure; no later root-corruption assertions in that method ran. XML/UTP/logcat/checksums are retained at `results/PRODUCT/ENG-PDF-IMPORT-CORPUS-0.4.195/focused/attempt-1/`.
- Source audit confirms `withDocument` calls `PDDocument.load` before checking `document.isEncrypted`; a password-required document raises `com.tom_roush.pdfbox.pdmodel.encryption.InvalidPasswordException`, which is an `IOException` in the pinned PDFBox Android 2.0.27.0 AAR. `inspect()` currently catches that under the generic malformed/unreadable `IOException` handler. An encrypted document with an empty user password reaches `isEncrypted` and already receives the unsupported wording, but that second encryption branch has no focused corpus case.
- **Class ENG-PDF-IMPORT-CORPUS-01-F1 (P/F/E):** distinguish password-required encrypted input from malformed/unreadable input, while continuing to reject every encrypted PDF. In the shared `withDocument` path, map `InvalidPasswordException` to `PdfImportInvalidSourceException("Encrypted PDFs are not supported for import", cause)` before the generic IOException path; map a successfully loaded `document.isEncrypted` document to the same explicit exception. Do not prompt for passwords or accept encrypted PDFs. Add both password-required and empty-user-password encrypted fixtures. Verify `inspect` and `renderPages` give explicit failure, no page callback or scratch residue, stable source hashes and a successful later open of the valid control. Keep malformed, structural-root, zero-page, truncation and all renderer cleanup controls unchanged.
- Implementation target advances to product `.196`/code 315. Run the focused PDF reader class first; its complete corpus must pass before full API 36.1 connected suite, exact-source `tools/check_all.sh --rerun`, release identity/signature and `.195`→`.196` replacement install/fresh-launch retention are evaluated. Preserve the `.195` failure unchanged. If the separately registered broken-root fixture reveals another product gap, treat it as an additional finding with a preregistered bounded fix.
- This corrects error classification for already-unsupported encrypted PDFs; it does not add encryption/password support or change page limits, repair tolerance, quality thresholds, product-task/CAP/gate/MVP status, or acceptance criteria. Parent T5.4/T5.5 and CAP-002 remain PARTIAL.

Execution contract (revision 5.304, 2026-09-27) — ENG-PDF-IMPORT-PAGE-PUBLISH-SYNC-01:
- Baseline product `.196`/315, source commit `8b5d1a9`; safety tag `before-ENG-PDF-IMPORT-PAGE-PUBLISH-SYNC-01`. Source audit: `FileArtifactRepository.publishBytes` atomically publishes the deterministic image target before `syncDirectoryChain`; an IOException from the directory sync can leave the image target present while `ProductStorage.materializePdfImport` has not yet written its `ArtifactRecord` or Page. Its error path records the ImportSession failure, and `publishOrVerifyImage` is intended to reuse only byte-identical targets on retry. This is a source-derived risk hypothesis; runtime behavior for this boundary is unobserved.
- Product scenario: a user imports a multi-page PDF and a transient filesystem I/O failure occurs while making one rendered page image durable. Already committed prefix pages must remain stable; the failed page must not appear as completed before its metadata/Page transaction; retry must resume in source order without duplicate or lost pages.
- Scope: add one test-only API integration case using the existing `ProductStorage.directorySyncFactory` seam. Generate a three-page private PDF, permit source publication and page 0, then inject exactly one `IOException` at the directory sync for page 1's deterministic image shard after its final image target is present. This is a simulated sync I/O failure, not physical disk exhaustion and not a change to product failure policy. Target product `.197`/316.
- Fixed acceptance: (1) the source SHA is unchanged and the injected failure occurs exactly once after the page 1 PNG object exists; (2) the first import propagates the exact injected IOException, progress reports only page 0 (1/3), ImportSession remains OPEN with its error recorded, `Page.source_page_index` is exactly `[0]`, page 1 has no `ArtifactRecord`, its deterministic orphan file is a complete valid PNG, and no PDF-render scratch or artifact staging temp remains; (3) `resumeIncompletePdfImports` succeeds without reinjection, returns the exact three deterministic Page IDs, source indices `[0,1,2]` occur once each, page 1's `ArtifactRecord` matches the reused object bytes, ImportSession is COMPLETED with the error cleared, source SHA remains unchanged, and scheduler admission is reusable; (4) focused test passes 1/1, complete API 36 connected suite passes with zero failures/errors/skips, exact-source `tools/check_all.sh --rerun` exits 0, `.197` signed package identity verifies, and `.196`→`.197` fresh-launch update preserves the existing Page 1 and checked save selection. Preserve command/environment, JUnit XML, UTP/logcat, DB queries, object/staging/cache hashes and release-update UI evidence under `results/PRODUCT/ENG-PDF-IMPORT-PAGE-PUBLISH-SYNC-0.4.197/` and `execution/device_tests/0.4.197-current-api36/`; verify packet manifests.
- If the injected boundary is missed, the failed page commits, the object is partial/invalid, retry produces different bytes, or recovery does not converge, preserve the attempt and exact state; do not weaken the registered assertions. A demonstrated production defect requires a new finding/correction contract and another version increment before product-code edits.
- Boundary: this measures one simulated post-publication directory-sync IOException and deterministic recovery only. It does not prove actual ENOSPC behavior, full T5.5 low-storage/cancellation/memory/quality, T5.6's remaining crash/provider matrix, FACT-07, physical Xiaomi qualification or owner acceptance. Keep all task/CAP/gate/MVP statuses and thresholds unchanged.

If the generated source text is not reusable, its first retry schedules background OCR for the imported Pages; a second retry then competes for the single `HeavyWorkScheduler` admission slot while rendering its next page. The current packet does not independently establish the generated PDF's extracted text, queued OCR state, or admission wait, so do not treat this as a confirmed defect or change the production scheduler yet.
- Keep revision 5.304 behavioral and evidence assertions, revision 5.308–5.311 SAF scope, test timeout, and all task/CAP/gate/MVP statuses and acceptance thresholds unchanged. First re-run the isolated TryAgain method and preserve XML/UTP/logcat plus the observed trace. Execution result and fixture correction contract (revision 5.313, 2026-09-27) — ENG-PDF-IMPORT-PAGE-PUBLISH-SYNC-01:
- Product `.205`/324 diagnostic source is commit `ba4c5ba`; its isolated `TryAgainImportActivityInstrumentedTest.retryImportsAllPagesSuppressesConcurrentDuplicateAndPreservesSource` run compiled and failed 1/1 at the existing 30-second second-retry page-count assertion. The registered diagnostic log proves the two-page generated source PDF had `hasReusableTextLayer=false` for both pages. After the first retry, both imported Pages had `recognizedText=null` and were queued for OCR (one active). The second retry then alternated scheduler ownership: OCR held 30,899,008 decoded bytes; PDF rendering held 83,886,080 bytes; after 30,466 ms there were five Pages, the first new Page of the second retry had committed, and the remaining OCR job was active while the second PDF page had not committed. The final assertion snapshot at 31,053 ms was `workspacePages=5`, one first-retry Page recognized, one OCR queued/running, and scheduler reservation 30,899,008 bytes. No import exception or data-corruption result was observed; the existing bounded scheduler serialized work, but this test's 30-second wait expired during that serialization. Exact source/test snapshot, console, JUnit XML, UTP/logcat, diagnostic events and verified manifest: `results/PRODUCT/ENG-PDF-IMPORT-PAGE-PUBLISH-SYNC-0.4.205/diagnostic/attempt-1-retry-ocr-all-pages/`.
- Classify this as test-fixture interference with background OCR in the retry-identity test, not a confirmed production defect. The scenario declares a fully recognized recent PDF but its artificial initial recognition labels (`FIRST PAGE`, `SECOND PAGE`) are shorter than `PdfTextLayerPolicy`'s fixed 20-character minimum, so the actual exported PDF does not match that declared scenario and correctly schedules OCR. This diagnosis is evidence-based for `.205`'s generated fixture and scheduler state; no timing claim is made about physical devices.
- Preserve every existing retry identity, concurrent-duplicate suppression, selection, source-byte/hash assertion and the 30-second helper limits. For the next instrumentation-only target `.206`/325, keep the visible fixture labels but make the persisted recognized text and hidden PDF text layer representative of the `ocrStatus=FULL` scenario (at least 20 non-whitespace Latin/Cyrillic letters or digits); assert that both generated source pages actually satisfy `hasReusableTextLayer` before starting the retry actions, and assert the first retry's imported Pages retain that reusable text. Remove the temporary private-field reflection diagnostics. Do not alter product scheduler behavior, acceptance thresholds, task/CAP/gate/MVP statuses or retry ordering. This narrows the test to its stated page-retry/identity invariant while other OCR-specific tests continue to exercise OCR behavior.
- On exact `.206` source, rerun the focused TryAgain test and all affected SAF checks before a complete API 36 suite. Require the second retry to publish all pages within the unchanged helper window, keep the same Page/ImageVersion uniqueness and duplicate assertions, and verify no OCR jobs are left queued by this reusable-text fixture. Execution contract (revision 5.319, 2026-09-27) — ENG-PDF-SCRATCH-IO-FAILURE-01, next T5.5 low-storage error-path slice:
- Source inspection found that `AndroidPdfImportReader.renderPages` creates one PNG scratch file per page, opens/writes/syncs it with `FileOutputStream`, recycles the rendered bitmap in `finally`, deletes the current file in an outer `finally`, and propagates I/O failures. Existing renderer-failure and consumer-failure tests cover adjacent boundaries, but no focused test injects failure from the PNG output operation itself. This identifies a coverage gap; it is not evidence of an observed production defect or of actual ENOSPC behavior.
- At baseline product `.206`/325, add the smallest internal output-writer seam needed to inject `IOException` during opening, after a partial PNG write, and during sync. Leave the production `FileOutputStream`/PNG compression/`fd.sync()` path and all normal outputs unchanged. Advance to `.207`/326 because product/test source changes.
- Pre-registered negative oracle on a three-page source: run the three failure phases at the second page after page 1's successful callback. Each injected failure must propagate as `IOException`; only page 1 may reach the consumer; the failed and later pages must not be rendered or delivered; all returned bitmaps must be recycled exactly once; current-page scratch bytes must be deleted; source-PDF SHA and existing complete-prefix observation must remain unchanged; the PDF must reopen; and the shared admission reservation must be available immediately after failure. Preserve existing renderer/consumer failures, source corruption controls and all retry/selection assertions. The focused reader class must pass with zero failures/errors/skips.
- This contract verifies deterministic output I/O failure handling only. It does not claim physical low-storage/ENOSPC behavior, cancellation semantics, ProductActivity/database rollback beyond the bounded reader callback boundary, or memory/quality limits. No failure threshold, provider rule, product status, CAP/gate/MVP status or owner criterion changes.
- The step is accepted only after the focused `.207` reader class and complete API 36 connected suite pass with zero failures/errors/skips; clean exact-source `tools/check_all.sh --rerun` exits 0; signed `.206`→`.207` release identity and installed bytes match; the in-place AVD upgrade retains firstInstallTime, active Page 1 and checked save-selection after fresh launch; and XML/UTP/logcat/runner/APK/UI evidence manifests verify. Execution contract (revision 5.330, 2026-09-27) — ENG-PDF-SCRATCH-CANCELLATION-01, bounded T5.5 cancellation:
- **Authority and baseline:** existing Phase 5/T5.5 row in this plan and UFS; product `.208`/327, source/evidence commit `e947724`; safety tag `before-ENG-PDF-SCRATCH-CANCELLATION-01`. The user scenario is a multi-page PDF import canceled during page materialization: already committed prefix pages remain valid, the in-flight page is not published as committed, and a later resume completes without duplication. The inspected `AndroidPdfImportReader.renderPages` has no cancellation argument or cooperative checkpoints; `ProductStorage.importPdf` therefore cannot propagate cancellation to page rendering. This is a source observation, not yet a demonstrated product failure.
- **Scope:** product `.209`/328. Add an Android `CancellationSignal` through `ProductStorage.importPdf`/page materialization into `AndroidPdfImportReader.renderPages`. Define bounded checkpoints before each page rasterization, immediately after rasterizer returns, before PNG output, and before invoking page consumer. A cancellation after the rasterizer returns must recycle the bitmap exactly once, close output if opened, remove the temporary PNG, propagate `OperationCanceledException`, preserve committed prefix/session state, and prevent current/later callbacks. Cancellation at a page boundary after one successful page must leave the import resumable; a retry without cancellation must yield exact unique ordered page IDs, no missing/duplicate pages, unchanged source SHA, no scratch residue and reusable scheduler. Preserve all existing renderer/I/O failure and positive geometry cases.
- **Test/evidence conditions:** add focused reader cancellation at an in-render checkpoint with resource cleanup and no later rendering/callback, plus an integration test through `ProductStorage.importPdf` canceled after the first durable page and successfully resumed. Require focused cases to prove page order/prefix, signal path, session state/error, image/artifact ownership, source SHA and retry outcome in structured logs/queries. Then require the entire AndroidPdfImportReader class and complete API 36 connected suite to pass with zero failures/errors/skips, exact-source `tools/check_all.sh --rerun` exit 0, signed `.208`→`.209` identity and retained workspace/selection after fresh launch, and verified evidence manifests. Keep exact source, command, device, environment, JUnit/UTP/logcat, DB/artifact/scratch observations and APK/UI evidence in `results/PRODUCT/ENG-PDF-SCRATCH-CANCELLATION-0.4.209/` and `execution/device_tests/0.4.209/`.
- **Boundaries and failure handling:** this is an API/runtime cancellation propagation slice; it adds no new cancel UI, storage threshold, cancellation policy after page commit, OCR cancellation semantics or claim about native PDFBox interruption mid-call. If `PDFRenderer` cannot be interrupted while inside its render call, record that bound and require cancellation immediately after return before any output/callback; do not claim immediate preemption. If cleanup, prefix preservation or recovery fails, retain the run and treat it as a demonstrated defect under a new correction contract/version. Do not relax criteria. No task/CAP/gate/MVP status changes; T5.5/CAP-002 and MVP remain partial/`IN_PROGRESS_NOT_ACCEPTED`.

Authority: прямое поручение владельца «по умолчанию PDF файлы сохранялись в /Docscan». Source HEAD269c5994, product0.4.232/351; target0.4.233/352. Новое требование не переоткрывает Native UI/PDF evidence. /Docscan означает папку Docscan в корне общей внутренней памяти (primary:Docscan), не Linux root. Android SAF требует explicit access: предлагаем эту папку в системном picker; если её нет, инструкция создать Docscan во внутренней памяти. Не обещаем автоматическое создание без разрешения. Существующую выбранную папку сохраняем; PDF+JPG package, no-overwrite, grant revalidation, qualification и success-only consumption неизменны. MediaStore/All-files access не вводятся.

## USABLE-SCAN-TO-PDF-01 — подробный план и контракт · 2026-10-16

**Authority / baseline:** прямое новое поручение владельца «Делай то что считаешь нужным… детальный план… агенты… приступай… самостоятельно… Save PDF не работает, даже не активна». Это новая продуктовая работа после DEFAULT .234, не повтор приёмки того пакета. HEAD d5257ccac5d1f94d1e8d693fe5b0c9b898fef53c, source5d5ad97e, version0.4.234/code353; tag before-usable-scan-to-pdf-0.4.234; branch codex/usable-scan-to-pdf. Чужие incoming app/runner/docs/evidence остаются вне собственных коммитов. Источники: UFS CAP-001/010/014, FM FINAL VERIFIED §§4–10,14–21, согласованные Native UI/DEFAULT решения, текущие T3.4/T6.1–6.7/T8.3. Внешний review — источник находок, не автоматическая замена критериев.

**Конечный пользовательский результат:** свежая установка позволяет снять/импортировать страницы, быстро принять следующие снимки, явно настроить /Docscan и сохранить проверенный PDF+JPG пакет через работающую Save PDF; ошибка не теряет исходные Pages; сохраняются selection/order/retake/Viewer/editor/recovery. Одновременно открыт один продуктовый пакет этого сквозного результата; шаги ниже сохраняются отдельными source-коммитами с подъёмом версии. Первый резерв .235/code354; далее следующий свободный номер по фактическим исходникам/артефактам. Не вводить 50мс SLA, два heavy workers, новый capture cap100, произвольный Unicode support либо автоматическое удаление неизвестных файлов.

### Архитектура и существенный конфликт

**USABLE-D01 / Save readiness:** прежняя политика T6.1/T6.7/.016 запрещала Save без persisted exact-tuple qualification. Диагноз Luna: production никогда не создаёт такую запись для реального provider, self-test делает только изолированный sandbox, положительные UI tests вставляют TEST_FIXTURE_ONLY. Поэтому обычный пользователь навсегда получает disabled Save. Новое прямое поручение владельца исправить этот пользовательский blocker — authority для изменения операционной доступности, а не отмены frozen S5/physical GateQ. Выбранное решение: Save как доступное действие при1..100 выбранных Pages и незанятом workspace; до любого export пройти one-time folder/access setup и настоящую ограниченную проверку capabilities текущего primary tree. Не выполнять freeze/create user outputs без успешных checks. Результат сохраняется с явным происхождением RUNTIME_CAPABILITY_CHECK, exact provider/package/version/build/access/tree, suite/evidence; formal S5/physical qualification=false. Existing formal/legacy records сохраняют проверенные границы. Operational readiness отдельно от formal acceptance; ни physical400-scenario S5, ни GateQ, ни owner acceptance этим не закрываются. Меню и Save/editor используют один readiness-механизм. Cloud/out-of-scope tree дают понятный отказ/смену папки, не создаётся фиктивная qualification.

**USABLE-D02 / Capture:** bounded file-backed intake, стабильный ID/порядок на принятии; CameraX и публикация асинхронны, commit serial в порядке принятия, OCR только enqueue после commit. Page видна только после durable artifact+metadata commit. Очередь ограничивает незавершённые запросы и сообщает backpressure; это не лимит workspace. Retake pinned PageId/revision. Worker/heavy-memory profile остаётся1. Размер очереди — документированный инженерный default, не performance acceptance threshold; фактический latency/PSS/thermal только измеряется на применимом устройстве.

**USABLE-D03 / PDF+SAF:** bundled licensed font для заявленных рус/анг, preflight coverage/encoding до публикации; unsupported text даёт точный recoverable отказ с сохранёнными Pages, без фильтрации символов/ослабления text readback. No geometry/image-only сохраняет прежнюю явную политику. SAF returned URI/name сохраняются/проверяются до stream write; rename/mismatch не получает пользовательские bytes, остаётся учтённой неопределённостью без повторной записи/автоудаления. Recovery читает durable created identity в первую очередь и подтверждает planned name+size+SHA; разрешение ambiguous create не подменять guess по совпавшему чужому файлу.

**USABLE-D04 / UI:** visible Home rail на RecyclerView/stable PageId, off-main bounded thumbnail loader/cache с version/size key, stale-binding fence; detailed Menu строится по необходимости, не на каждом hidden update. Нижнее положение всех эскизов,172dp Home block, inset/blur,48dp/a11y, selection≠active, order/Undo/dirty guards сохраняются. Existing PDF24-image cache/Viewer не переписывать без причины. HOCR→ResultIterator — только после наблюдаемого resource gap и parity/measurement; не обязательный беспричинный rewrite.

## UI-HTML-PROTOTYPE-04 — нижняя лента и inset-просмотр · 2026-10-16

**Authority / baseline:** прямое поручение владельца к `prototypes/docscan-screens.html`: с третьего экрана и далее эскизы должны оставаться в нижней зоне; открытый документ должен иметь заметные отступы со всех сторон, а изображение приложения под ним — быть слегка затемнено и размыто. Исходник `.4.4` / commit `153dc789`; отдельный локальный HTML-прототип, не Android-пакет `.237`. Рабочее дерево `prototypes/` чистое; unrelated dirty Android/runner/docs/evidence остаются чужими и вне staging.

**Scope / invariants:** изменить только общий viewer layout для одиночной страницы и сохранённого PDF: порядок верхнего заголовка, действий, документа, его навигации/инструментов и нижней strip; inset viewer; общий backdrop. Полный inventory экранов с viewer strip: `review`, `crop`, `brightness`, `contrast`, `sharpness`, `text`, `dirty-guard`, `low-quality`, `ocr-error`, `pdf-viewer`, `pdf-edit`, `pdf-save`. Для всех остальных 20 сцен сохраняются маршруты, состав, текст, порядок элементов и поведение; главная/пустая камера сохраняет свою нижнюю ленту; retake сохраняет камеру и ряд снятых страниц над управлением камерой. Не менять editor/OCR/selection/undo/save/import/permissions/PDF semantics, содержимое эскизов, остальные подписи/элементы, APK, runner, критерии продукта и статусы.

**Finite acceptance / evidence:** `.4.6` build + syntax; browser run по всем 32 routes/captures; на каждом из 12 viewer IDs strip расположен после документа и действий в нижней зоне, viewer имеет заметный inset по четырём краям, backdrop получает умеренное затемнение и blur; по 20 остальным IDs сохраняются исходные элементы/поведение; page/PDF swipe, selection, crop touch, dirty guard и Save actions работают. Сравнить screenshots `.4.4`/`.4.6`: допустимые изменения ограничены viewer screens (новая нижняя лента, inset/backdrop) и стандартным обновлением version display; у остальных экранов визуальное содержимое/положение контролов должно совпадать. Сохранить raw verification, 32 captures и manifest в `prototypes/evidence/0.4.6/`; тестировать только prototype, не Android/device/release. Шаг принят при точном version/build/test evidence и проверке diff изображений с указанным affected set; real camera/OCR/SAF/PDF и device acceptance остаются NOT RUN, независимое visual review — REVIEW_REQUIRED.


### UI-HTML-PROTOTYPE-04 — результат .4.6 · 2026-10-17

Прямой визуальный остаток UI-HTML-PROTOTYPE-03 реализован в standalone HTML-прототипе `.4.6`; baseline `.4.4`, base commit `153dc789`. `renderViewer` выводит общий page/PDF thumbnail strip последним дочерним блоком после документа, навигации и инструментов. В обычном и presentation режиме viewer inset 18px по сторонам и 8% по вертикали; общий backdrop имеет `rgba(8,12,18,.47)` и `blur(4px)`. Главная/пустая camera, retake controls, остальные составы/тексты/actions и Android не изменялись.

**Version namespace:** передаваемая версия назначена `.4.6`, потому что checkout уже содержит архивированный abandoned experiment `.4.5` (commit `b6f34f01`); исходный `prototypes/evidence/0.4.5/` восстановлен байт-в-байт из HEAD и не перезаписывается. Первый диагностический run с metadata `.4.5` сохранён отдельно в `prototypes/evidence/0.4.6/attempt-1-pre-version-bump/`.

**Independent audit amendment:** GPT-6 Luna/high independently inspected source, SHA-256 entries, all 32 screenshots, pixel-diff counts and the unchanged archived `.4.5` evidence. The audit found the initial browser oracle accepted smaller minimum insets than the CSS/evidence specified. The oracle now asserts exact 18px side insets, 8% top/bottom (within 0.1px), a 1px viewer border gap, `rgba(8, 12, 18, 0.47)` and `blur(4px)`. This aligns verification with the recorded layout; it changes no product scope. Luna did not execute browser behavior; owner visual review remains open.

**Evidence / исполнение:** `python3 prototypes/build-prototype.py` PASS; `node --check prototypes/docscan.js` и `node --check prototypes/verify-prototype.cjs` PASS. После усиления геометрических assertions выполнен полный bounded `NODE_PATH=/Users/restart/.cache/codex-runtimes/codex-primary-runtime/dependencies/node/node_modules node prototypes/verify-prototype.cjs` на Google Chrome `155.0.8059.82`, Node `v22.21.0`, macOS: **78/78 PASS**, **32/32 captures**, runtime errors 0. Все 12 viewer routes проходят exact assertions: `.top-strip` — последний дочерний блок, горизонтальные поля 18px, вертикальные 8% (63.76px при appHeight797, измерено 63.75px), нижний зазор 1px, backdrop `rgba(8, 12, 18, 0.47)`/`blur(4px)`. Compact main tray/actions остался 159px без overflow. Повторный pixel diff `.4.4`→`.4.6` (`prototypes/evidence/0.4.6/visual-diff.json`): 12 ожидаемо изменённых viewer routes, 18 pixel-identical routes, `about`/`self-check` меняют только видимый номер версии. Первый диагностический run до перенумерации (metadata `.4.5`) остановился на ошибочном `<1px` assertion для рамки; raw сохранён в `prototypes/evidence/0.4.6/attempt-1-pre-version-bump/`. Assertion исправлен на ожидаемую 1px границу и точные insets; итоговый полный `.4.6` run прошёл.

Галерея `prototypes/docscan-screens.html` и standalone app пересобраны с той же версией; итоговая галерея открывает встроенные сцены офлайн, gallery runtime/dirty-close guard проходят в 78-check прогоне. Полный P/F/C/E, limitations и evidence links — `prototypes/README.md`; source/evidence checksums — `prototypes/evidence/SHA256SUMS`. Камера/OCR/Android picker/SAF/permissions/PDF writes и device qualification по-прежнему симуляции/NOT RUN. Независимое визуальное review владельца **REVIEW_REQUIRED**; Android `.237` owner-paused package, APK/runner/frozen criteria/CAP/gate/MVP не изменялись. Следующий шаг — review обновлённого обзора владельцем.

## PROD-LIVE-DOC-DETECTION-05 — Режим «Документ» и редизайн вьюера (v0.4.243) · 2026-10-17

**Authority:** прямое распоряжение владельца от 2026-10-17 (Edge Detection + Perspective Correction + Редизайн окна просмотра/редактирования).
**Scope:**
1. **Режим камеры «Документ»:**
   - Кнопка-тумблер «Документ» справа от кнопки «Пуск» с зеленой индикацией активного состояния (`#22C55E`).
   - Потоковая детекция контура листа в реальном времени через `CameraX ImageAnalysis` (троттлинг ~150 мс) и OpenCV (`detectCornersFromRgba`).
   - Зеленый оверлей-полигон `DocumentOverlayView` (цвет `#22C55E`, stroke 3dp, маркеры углов, полупрозрачная заливка 15%).
   - Автоматическая коррекция перспективы (гомография `warpPerspective`) при съёмке с активным режимом.
2. **Модернизация окна просмотра и редактирования (`showPageViewer`):**
   - Удаление надписей с кнопок ROTATE, CROP, ADJUST — оставлены только современные векторные иконки (`ic_rotate_right`, `ic_crop`, `ic_tune`).
   - Переименование кнопок действий: «Переснять» $\rightarrow$ `ReClick`, «РАСПОЗНАТЬ» $\rightarrow$ `ReOCR`, «УДАЛИТЬ» $\rightarrow$ контурная иконка корзины (`ic_delete_trash`).
   - Увеличение размера эскизов в ленте на **+17%** (со 88×64 dp до 103×75 dp).
   - Динамический размер зоны текста OCR: контейнер `ocrScroll` с `minHeight = 42dp`, `maxHeight = 130dp` и внутренним скроллом.
   - Адаптивный размер диалога в `InsetDialogHost` (`WRAP_CONTENT` с системными отступами).
**Evidence:**
- Сборка: `assembleRelease` и `assembleDebug` успешны.
- Готовые файлы: `dist/DocScan-release-0.4.243.apk` (52 МБ) и `app/build/outputs/apk/debug/DocScan-debug-0.4.243.apk` (196 МБ).
